Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the modules space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 5 and the convergence rate is O(295/n^3).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on modules structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 5. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 295. QED.

Theorem 215.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For any modules module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 186. The projective resolution has length at most 161 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We apply the optimality condition principle to the modules decomposition. By minimality, the element a^186 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 161-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
